    var allowSaveCookie = null;

    (function (window, $) {

        var $body = $(document.body),
            $window = $(window);

        function isApp() {
            return !!navigator.userAgent.match(/stylebop/i);
        }

        if (isApp() === true) {
            $body.find('#notice-cookie-block').remove();
            $body.find('#notice-japanese-block').remove();
        }

        if (Mage.Cookies.get('user_allowed_save_cookie')) {
            $body.find('#notice-cookie-block').remove();
        } else {
            $body.find('#notice-cookie-block').addClass('show-cookie');
        }
        $window.resize();

        window.allowSaveCookie = function () {
            Mage.Cookies.set('user_allowed_save_cookie', '1', new Date(new Date().getTime() + 31536000000));
            if (!Mage.Cookies.get('user_allowed_save_cookie')) {
                window.location.href = 'https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/cms/index/noCookies/';
            } else {
                $body.find('#notice-cookie-block').remove();

            }
            $window.resize();
        }

    })(window, jQuery);
</script>

<h1 class="h1 special">STYLEBOP.COM</h1>
<h2 class="h3 special">ONLINE LUXURY FASHION FOR WOMEN</h2>
</div>
<p>Founded in 2004, STYLEBOP.com is a global leader in luxury e-commerce and the digital home to over <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/designers.html" title="Women's Designer Fashion">200 international designer fashion brands</a> – spanning across <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/clothing.html" title="Women’s Designer Clothing">womenswear</a>, <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/shoes.html" title="Designer Shoes">footwear</a>, <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/bags.html" title="Designer Bags">bags</a>, <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/accessories.html" title="Shop Designer Accessories">accessories</a>, and <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/accessories/jewelry.html" title="Shop luxury jewelry online">jewelry</a>. Providing endless inspiration for the current season, our unparalleled edit is updated several times a week, with highly coveted <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/new-arrivals/this-week.html" title="New Designer Fashion">New Arrivals</a> showcased across a complete style universe that includes the latest runway trends, site-exclusive editorial content and multilingual customer service.</p>
<p class="seo-text-content hidden">From the onset, STYLEBOP.com has championed a truly curated point of view, which is implemented by our unique buying strategy. Top-tier luxury brands are expertly juxtaposed against emerging and contemporary labels to craft an exciting and unparalleled selection and product mix. This singular edit captures our own ethos of creativity and innovation, while addressing the modern customer’s desire for a complete lifestyle wardrobe. This personalized approach to luxury is built on strong relationships with individual brands, which has also yielded unique creative and merchandising opportunities, from special partnerships to online exclusives. This allows us to consistently deliver an offer to our customers that is truly engaging and distinct - and which they can't find anywhere else.</p>

<div class="seo-headlines-container">
<p class="h3 special">STYLEBOP</p>
<p class="h2 special">The Latest In Women's Fashion</p>
</div>
<p>The Spring/Summer 2019 Womenswear Collections have arrived! STYLEBOP.com has been scouring the runways for the most coveted trends of the upcoming season. Now at your fingertips, we’ve translated our favorites into exciting and inspiring edits. And as the days get longer and winter fades, there’s no time like the present to stock up on colorful, on-point pieces. Discover everything you need on STYLEBOP! Flowing fabric and light-as-air drapes are in focus, while florals of all kinds are here to stay. Color is as bright as ever in primaries, red and yellow, and softer shades make an appearance in softer hues like lilac. <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/shoes/sneakers.html" title="Designer Sneakers for Women">Sneakers</a> are also ever-present, with an <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/clothing/athletic-wear.html" title="Athletic Wear for Women">Athleisure</a> outlook on comfort – think designer <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/clothing/athletic-wear/trousers.html" title="Sweatpants for Women">sweats</a>, <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/clothing/athletic-wear/hoodies.html" title="Hoodies for Women">oversized hoodies</a> and <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/clothing/t-shirts.html" title="T-Shirts for Women">sporty tees</a>. The midi length is especially popular, and for full effect, we’re teaming it with animal prints, patterns and bright colors. From <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/clothing/dresses/midi-dresses.html" title="Midi Dresses for Women">dresses</a> to <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/clothing/skirts/midi-maxi-skirts.html" title="Midi and Maxi Skirts for Women">skirts</a>, find out how you like it best with our diverse selection from top designers. And naturally, no outfit would be complete without the perfect pair of (open!) <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/shoes.html" title="Designer Shoes for Women">shoes</a>. We’re indulging in every style – from <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/shoes/sandals.html" title="Designer Sandals for Women">sandals</a> to <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/shoes/espadrilles.html" title="Espadrilles for Women">espadrilles</a>, <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/shoes/mules.html" title="Mules for Women">mules</a>  to <a href="https://www.stylebop.com/en-de/women/shoes/sneakers.html" title="Designer Sneakers for Women">sneakers</a>. Offering you the best of what Spring/Summer has to offer, STYLEBOP has you covered from head to toe!</p>
